entries = [<DirEntry 'transfer.py'>, <DirEntry 'practice.py'>, <DirEntry 'heldout.py'>, <DirEntry 'sample.py'>]
src = PosixPath('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials')
dst = PosixPath('/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials')
symlinks = False, ignore = None
copy_function = <function copy2 at 0x7fc213425a80>
ignore_dangling_symlinks = False, dirs_exist_ok = False

    def _copytree(entries, src, dst, symlinks, ignore, copy_function,
                  ignore_dangling_symlinks, dirs_exist_ok=False):
        if ignore is not None:
            ignored_names = ignore(os.fspath(src), [x.name for x in entries])
        else:
            ignored_names = ()
    
        os.makedirs(dst, exist_ok=dirs_exist_ok)
        errors = []
        use_srcentry = copy_function is copy2 or copy_function is copy
    
        for srcentry in entries:
            if srcentry.name in ignored_names:
                continue
            srcname = os.path.join(src, srcentry.name)
            dstname = os.path.join(dst, srcentry.name)
            srcobj = srcentry if use_srcentry else srcname
            try:
                is_symlink = srcentry.is_symlink()
                if is_symlink and os.name == 'nt':
                    # Special check for directory junctions, which appear as
                    # symlinks but we want to recurse.
                    lstat = srcentry.stat(follow_symlinks=False)
                    if lstat.st_reparse_tag == stat.IO_REPARSE_TAG_MOUNT_POINT:
                        is_symlink = False
                if is_symlink:
                    linkto = os.readlink(srcname)
                    if symlinks:
                        # We can't just leave it to `copy_function` because legacy
                        # code with a custom `copy_function` may rely on copytree
                        # doing the right thing.
                        os.symlink(linkto, dstname)
                        copystat(srcobj, dstname, follow_symlinks=not symlinks)
                    else:
                        # ignore dangling symlink if the flag is on
                        if not os.path.exists(linkto) and ignore_dangling_symlinks:
                            continue
                        # otherwise let the copy occur. copy2 will raise an error
                        if srcentry.is_dir():
                            copytree(srcobj, dstname, symlinks, ignore,
                                     copy_function, ignore_dangling_symlinks,
                                     dirs_exist_ok)
                        else:
                            copy_function(srcobj, dstname)
                elif srcentry.is_dir():
                    copytree(srcobj, dstname, symlinks, ignore, copy_function,
                             ignore_dangling_symlinks, dirs_exist_ok)
                else:
                    # Will raise a SpecialFileError for unsupported file types
                    copy_function(srcobj, dstname)
            # catch the Error from the recursive copytree so that we can
            # continue with other files
            except Error as err:
                errors.extend(err.args[0])
            except OSError as why:
                errors.append((srcname, dstname, str(why)))
        try:
            copystat(src, dst)
        except OSError as why:
            # Copying file access times may fail on Windows
            if getattr(why, 'winerror', None) is None:
                errors.append((src, dst, str(why)))
        if errors:
>           raise Error(errors)
E           shutil.Error: [('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/transfer.py', '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/transfer.py', "[Errno 28] No space left on device: '/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/transfer.py' -> '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/transfer.py'"), ('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/practice.py', '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/practice.py', "[Errno 28] No space left on device: '/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/practice.py' -> '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/practice.py'"), ('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/heldout.py', '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/heldout.py', "[Errno 28] No space left on device: '/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/heldout.py' -> '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/heldout.py'"), ('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/sample.py', '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/sample.py', "[Errno 28] No space left on device: '/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials/sample.py' -> '/dev/shm/n407full/test_fresh_is_explicit_and_pre0/standalone host/materials/sample.py'")]

entries = [<DirEntry 'transfer.py'>, <DirEntry 'practice.py'>, <DirEntry 'heldout.py'>, <DirEntry 'sample.py'>]
src = PosixPath('/workspace/nana-unified-release/packaging/portable/materials')
dst = PosixPath('/dev/shm/n407full/test_secret_memory_fd_and_miss0/standalone host/materials')
symlinks = False, ignore = None
copy_function = <function copy2 at 0x7fc213425a80>
ignore_dangling_symlinks = False, dirs_exist_ok = False

    def _copytree(entries, src, dst, symlinks, ignore, copy_function,
                  ignore_dangling_symlinks, dirs_exist_ok=False):
        if ignore is not None:
            ignored_names = ignore(os.fspath(src), [x.name for x in entries])
        else:
            ignored_names = ()
    
        os.makedirs(dst, exist_ok=dirs_exist_ok)
        errors = []
        use_srcentry = copy_function is copy2 or copy_function is copy
    
        for srcentry in entries:
            if srcentry.name in ignored_names:
                continue
            srcname = os.path.join(src, srcentry.name)
            dstname = os.path.join(dst, srcentry.name)
            srcobj = srcentry if use_srcentry else srcname
            try:
                is_symlink = srcentry.is_symlink()
                if is_symlink and os.name == 'nt':
                    # Special check for directory junctions, which appear as
                    # symlinks but we want to recurse.
                    lstat = srcentry.stat(follow_symlinks=False)
                    if lstat.st_reparse_tag == stat.IO_REPARSE_TAG_MOUNT_POINT:
                        is_symlink = False
                if is_symlink:
                    linkto = os.readlink(srcname)
                    if symlinks:
                        # We can't just leave it to `copy_function` because legacy
                        # code with a custom `copy_function` may rely on copytree
                        # doing the right thing.
                        os.symlink(linkto, dstname)
                        copystat(srcobj, dstname, follow_symlinks=not symlinks)
                    else:
                        # ignore dangling symlink if the flag is on
                        if not os.path.exists(linkto) and ignore_dangling_symlinks:
                            continue
                        # otherwise let the copy occur. copy2 will raise an error
                        if srcentry.is_dir():
                            copytree(srcobj, dstname, symlinks, ignore,
                                     copy_function, ignore_dangling_symlinks,
                                     dirs_exist_ok)
                        else:
                            copy_function(srcobj, dstname)
                elif srcentry.is_dir():
                    copytree(srcobj, dstname, symlinks, ignore, copy_function,
                             ignore_dangling_symlinks, dirs_exist_ok)
                else:
                    # Will raise a SpecialFileError for unsupported file types
                    copy_function(srcobj, dstname)
            # catch the Error from the recursive copytree so that we can
            # continue with other files
            except Error as err:
                errors.extend(err.args[0])
            except OSError as why:
                errors.append((srcname, dstname, str(why)))
        try:
            copystat(src, dst)
        except OSError as why:
            # Copying file access times may fail on Windows
            if getattr(why, 'winerror', None) is None:
                errors.append((src, dst, str(why)))
        if errors:
>           raise Error(errors)

    def projection(self,*,scopes,privacy_classes,now_us):
     b=self.binding
     # Scope/privacy before opening even a byte of the source.
     if b.scope not in scopes or b.privacy_class not in privacy_classes:return None
     try:
      path=self.root/b.relative_path
      if self.root.resolve()!=self.root or path.resolve()!=path:raise ValueError()
      fd=os.open(path,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_NONBLOCK)
      with os.fdopen(fd,'rb') as stream:
       if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(stream.fileno()).st_mode):raise ValueError()
       prefix=stream.read(PREFIX_LIMIT)
      begin=prefix.find(START)
      if not 0<=begin<=512 or prefix.count(START)!=1:raise ValueError()
      end=prefix.find(END,begin+len(START))
      if end<0:raise ValueError()
      raw=prefix[begin+len(START):end].strip()
      if len(raw)>8192:raise ValueError()
      state=ProjectCurrentState.model_validate_json(json.dumps(json.loads(raw,object_pairs_hook=_json_object)))
      if state.scope!=b.scope or state.privacy_class!=b.privacy_class:raise ValueError()
      if state.as_of_us>now_us or now_us-state.as_of_us>b.max_age_us:raise CoreError('PROJECT_CURRENT_STATE_STALE')
     except CoreError:raise
>    except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError):raise CoreError('PROJECT_CURRENT_STATE_UNAVAILABLE') from None
